# Household hh_s4 — 28 days, Monday to Sunday

Seed-generated household. Times are clock times; ids are the receptacle and object ids used in events.jsonl. A line indented under an activity says where an object went when the activity ended and why. WHIM marks a placement that landed somewhere other than where the decision was heading. Text in [brackets] names the hidden cause the decision is attributed to.

## Day 7 — Monday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d7`: Priya hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:27–16:03).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_3:d7`: Hana is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (12:26–14:26).
- `running_late:resident_2`: Dana is running late all day (0.95): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Priya energy 0.92, hurriedness 0.52, distraction 0.57; Dana energy 0.48, hurriedness 0.95, distraction 0.61; Hana energy 0.36, hurriedness 0.34, distraction 0.68

## Day 12 — Saturday

**Active causes**

- `ep:afternoon_slump:resident_1:d12`: Priya hits an afternoon slump: things get left where they were used for a couple of hours (14:44–16:59).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_2:d12`: Dana is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (15:49–16:19).
- `ep:craving_a_snack:resident_3:d12`: Hana is craving a snack and fits one in when there is a gap; the bowl and glass tend to be left out (16:12–16:42).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_2:d12`: Dana is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (13:05–15:05).

## Day 27 — Sunday

**Active causes**

- `ep:sore_back:resident_3:d27`: Hana has a sore back today and sits in the armchair instead of the couch, so things land on the side table (06:30–24:00).
- `ep:tidy_mood:resident_2:d27`: Dana is in a tidying mood for a while: a quick tidy of the living room, and things go back where they belong (13:58–15:58).
- `running_late:resident_1`: Priya is running late all day (0.75): rushed putdowns, things dumped at the door, more whim.

Internal states (0–1): Priya energy 0.63, hurriedness 0.75, distraction 0.44; Dana energy 0.48, hurriedness 0.50, distraction 0.48; Hana energy 0.33, hurriedness 0.70, distraction 0.55
